"""Source130: pinned Fudan JUMP formulas adapted to own MOD4 step geometry.

The historical filename is an explicit overlay. This is not source128.
Original126 success/process memory is independent of dense jump encouragement.
No action, force, reset, trajectory controller or physical success override.
"""
import torch
from direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 import CommandReward,smooth01

UPSTREAM_COMMIT='8204e853dfd2ed06d85a322e1a998c3d20a3be2c'


def own_wheel_height_score(bottom2,step_height=.27):
    """Fudan exp(-6*abs(height-target)), in actual wheel-bottom coordinates.

    Remove its floor offset and normalize BEFORE scaling to the original
    per-term2.5 cap. Thus zero at floor and strictly increasing to270mm;
    the original6*exp then clip would saturate well BEFORE its target.
    """
    floor=bottom2.new_tensor(-6*step_height).exp()
    each=((torch.exp(-6*(bottom2-step_height).abs())-floor)/(1-floor)).clamp(0,1)
    return each.amin(-1)


def evaluate(m,mode,legacy_nonrequest,effort_quality,*,launched,crossed,closure_m,public_age,p=CommandReward()):
    P=mode['top_support'];v=m['world_velocity3'];active=m['request_active']
    landing=P*crossed.to(P.dtype)
    desired_x=mode['base_command_vx']*(1-landing)
    error=(v[...,0]-desired_x).square()+v[...,1].square()
    # Original tracking and enhanced kernels/weights. Limit the enhanced cost
    # to its matching positive tracker: a bad command cannot make total reward
    # negative and reward early death. This is a declared safety adaptation.
    tracking=2*torch.exp(-error/.25)
    enhanced=torch.maximum(2*(torch.exp(-error/2.5)-1),-tracking)
    heading=(1-m['heading_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)
    alignment=torch.exp(-heading/(1-.9800665778412416)-m['root_y'].square()/.3**2)
    leading_front=m['wheel_high2'][...,0].amax(-1)
    before_edge=smooth01((p.edge_x-leading_front)/.04)
    bottom=m['wheel_bottom2'];minimum=bottom.amin(-1)
    H=own_wheel_height_score(bottom,p.step_height)
    clearance=(minimum/p.step_height).clamp(0,1)
    # Current observable geometry + two-control wheel-force history surrogate.
    # NO ready prerequisite, no16-callback prerequisite, no vx/near prerequisite.
    # This permits dense takeoff learning in the actual observed ready=0 branch.
    # It never changes the stricter original physical/process qualification.
    air=smooth01(minimum/.005)*smooth01(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']/.005)*m['unloaded_both']
    stage=torch.maximum(air,launched.to(v.dtype))
    # Preserve height/tuck credit through a qualified partial-footprint landing.
    # Otherwise first real top contact would erase the air terms before P rises.
    region=m['task_lane_and_far_bound'].to(v.dtype)
    jump=(1-P)*region
    upward=v[...,2].clamp(0,2.4)  # original.15*vz+, bounded at inherited engineering reference
    own_lift=torch.minimum(m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'],m['wheel_centre_velocity2']).amin(-1)
    conservative_up=torch.minimum(v[...,2],own_lift).clamp_min(0)
    # Before either wheel reaches the riser, real common wheel/root upward
    # velocity can encourage lift-off before height/history declares flight.
    # This is NOT flight qualification. Body-up with fixed wheels gives u=0.
    launch_clear=(leading_front<p.edge_x).to(v.dtype)*smooth01(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']/.005)
    line_precredit=(6*conservative_up).clamp_max(2.5)*launch_clear
    line_z=(6*conservative_up).clamp_max(2.5)*torch.maximum(air,launch_clear)
    length=m['virtual_leg_length2']
    extend=torch.exp(-4*(length-m['own_extension_length2']).abs().sum(-1))
    tuck=torch.exp(-4*(length-m['own_tuck_length2']).abs().sum(-1))
    # Preserve Fudan's support + root-vz>.15 takeoff definition, with measured
    # support history as an explicitly imperfect proxy. No mandatory crouch.
    # Transfer by reward amount: pre-air line credit replaces the same amount
    # of supported extension. Their loaded sum is max(extension,line_precredit).
    # Low wheel speed can leave a flat extension-dominant interval; no extra
    # history-switch valley is introduced where the133 total did not drop.
    takeoff=m['supported_any']*(v[...,2]>.15).to(v.dtype)*before_edge
    extension_credit=torch.relu(.5*extend*takeoff-line_precredit)
    quiet=torch.exp(-v.square().sum(-1)/.03**2-m['world_angular_velocity3'].square().sum(-1)/.3**2
        -(1-m['upright_cos'].clamp(-1,1)).clamp_min(0)/(1-.984807753012208)
        -heading/(1-.9800665778412416))
    dt=.01*effort_quality
    quality=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()*(public_age>=10).to(v.dtype)
    terms=dict(fudan_tracking_lin_vel=dt*tracking*alignment*active,
        fudan_tracking_lin_vel_enhance=dt*enhanced*alignment*active,
        fudan_tracking_ang_vel=dt*torch.exp(-m['body_yaw_velocity'].square()/.25)*active,
        fudan_encourage_jump_upward=dt*.15*upward*jump*active,
        fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry=dt*extension_credit*jump*active,
        fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent=dt*line_z*jump*active,
        fudan_flight_real_clearance=dt*.15*stage*clearance*jump*active,
        fudan_base_height_flight_own_wheel270=dt*2.5*H*stage*jump*active,
        fudan_leg_tuck_own_geometry=dt*1.7*tuck*stage*clearance*jump*active,
        supported_top_after_qualified_cross=dt*12*landing*active,
        quiet_top_after_qualified_cross=dt*4*landing*quiet*active,
        closure_compliance_cost=torch.zeros_like(dt),
        original_nonrequest_recovery=(1-active)*legacy_nonrequest)
    positive=sum(value for name,value in terms.items() if name not in ('closure_compliance_cost','original_nonrequest_recovery'))
    terms['closure_compliance_cost']=-.5*quality*positive
    total=sum(terms.values())
    if not bool(torch.isfinite(total).all()):raise ValueError('nonfinite130 reward')
    return dict(total=total,selected_terms=terms,command_vx=desired_x,
        command_wheel_bottom_height=.27,command_wheel_bottom_upward_velocity=torch.full_like(H,2.4),
        planning_top_support=P,qualified_cross=crossed,before_edge_gate=before_edge,
        both_wheel_height_score=H,closure_quality_cost=quality,
        dense_air_geometry_history_surrogate=air,dense_stage_with_qualified_landing_bridge=stage,
        true_wheel_clearance_fraction=clearance,conservative_upward_velocity=conservative_up,
        own_extension_length2=m['own_extension_length2'],own_tuck_length2=m['own_tuck_length2'],
        process_ready=m['process_ready'],dense_incentives_do_not_require_ready=True,
        process126_unchanged=True,upstream_commit=UPSTREAM_COMMIT,
        original_stateful_airtime_accumulator_not_selected=True,
        source_only_not_runtime_admitted=True,not_original_Fudan_two_policy_reward=True)


def combine_closure_risk(old_soft_done,closure_m,public_age):
    """Continuous engineering constraint-risk cost, not calibrated failure probability.

    The physical2mm guard is unchanged. Predicted1.2..1.8mm now has nonzero
    cost even though the WM underestimates the saved actual failure. This does
    not claim the underlying WM closure prediction has been repaired.
    """
    risk=(closure_m/.002).clamp(0,1).square()
    risk=torch.where(public_age>=10,risk,torch.zeros_like(risk))
    per_step=-torch.expm1(torch.log1p(-risk.clamp_max(1-1e-6))/32.)
    combined=1-(1-old_soft_done)*(1-per_step)
    return torch.where((public_age>=10)&(closure_m>.002),torch.ones_like(combined),combined)

    load=lambda p:json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
    sha=lambda p:hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    binding=load(folder/'INPUT_BINDINGS137.json')
    for row in binding['candidate_files']:assert sha(folder/row['name'])==row['sha256'],row['name']
    for row in binding['base_geometry_files']:assert sha(base/row['name'])==row['sha256'],row['name']
    for row in binding['baseline135_files']:assert sha(prior/row['name'])==row['sha256'],row['name']
    for row in binding['baseline136_files']:assert sha(prior136/row['name'])==row['sha256'],row['name']
    for row in binding['v2_files']:assert sha(v2/row['name'])==row['sha256'],row['name']
    provenance=load(prior/'ORIGINAL_SHA256.json');contract135=load(prior/'SOURCE_CONTRACT135.json')
    for row in provenance['inherited_runtime_sources']:
        assert sha(base/'source'/row['name'])==row['sha256'],row['name']
    report135=load(v2/'reports/ROOT135_V2_FIRST_DOMAIN_CORRECTED_FULL_CPU_ACTUAL.json')
    assert report135['status']=='PASS'
    failure136=load(prior136/'reports/ROOT136_FIRST_THREE_WAY_AMOUNT_TRANSFER_CPU_ACTUAL.json')
    probe136=load(prior136/'reports/ROOT136_NARROW_ZERO_BOUNDARY_SUBGRADIENT_SOURCE_CPU_ACTUAL.json')
    assert failure136['status']=='FAIL' and "ground_grad['136']" in failure136['traceback']
    assert probe136['status']=='SOURCE_DIAGNOSTIC_CONFIRMED_NOT_FULL_FIXTURE_PASS'
    PARTIAL_RESULTS['original136_failure_preserved']=True
    sources={'133':folder/'original133'/name,'135':folder/'original135'/name,
        '136':folder/'original136'/name,'137':folder/'source'/name}
    trees={k:ast.parse(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')) for k,p in sources.items()}
    function=lambda t:next(n for n in t.body if isinstance(n,ast.FunctionDef) and n.name=='evaluate')
    target=lambda n:n.targets[0].id if isinstance(n,ast.Assign) and len(n.targets)==1 and isinstance(n.targets[0],ast.Name) else None
    assignments=lambda tree:{target(n):n for n in function(tree).body if target(n)}
    a136,a137=(assignments(trees[k]) for k in ('136','137'))
    restored136=copy.deepcopy(trees['137'])
    for i,node in enumerate(function(restored136).body):
        if target(node)=='extension_credit':function(restored136).body[i]=copy.deepcopy(a136['extension_credit'])
    assert ast.dump(restored136)==ast.dump(trees['136']),'137 must differ from136 only at residual activation'
    assert ast.dump(a137['extension_credit'].value)==ast.dump(ast.parse(
        'torch.relu(.5*extend*takeoff-line_precredit)',mode='eval').body)
    assert ast.dump(a136['extension_credit'].value)==ast.dump(ast.parse(
        '(.5*extend*takeoff-line_precredit).clamp_min(0)',mode='eval').body)
    a135=assignments(trees['135']);a137=assignments(trees['137'])
    # terms is dict(keyword=...), not a literal dictionary.
    term_nodes=lambda a:{n.arg:n.value for n in a['terms'].value.keywords}
    t135,t137=term_nodes(a135),term_nodes(a137)
    assert t135.keys()==t137.keys()
    for k in t135:
        if k!='fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry':assert ast.dump(t135[k])==ast.dump(t137[k]),k
    restored=copy.deepcopy(trees['137']);restored_body=[]
    for node in function(restored).body:
        key=target(node)
        if key=='extension_credit':continue
        if key=='line_precredit':node=copy.deepcopy(a135['extension_exit'])
        elif key in ('takeoff','terms'):node=copy.deepcopy(a135[key])
        restored_body.append(node)
    function(restored).body=restored_body
    assert ast.dump(restored)==ast.dump(trees['135']),'Unexpected runtime change beyond amount transfer'
    rng_before=torch.get_rng_state().clone()
    sys.dont_write_bytecode=True;sys.path[:0]=[str(folder/'source'),str(base/'source')]
    import jump_process_command_reward122_v1 as selected
    import direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 as direct
    import jump_process_frame122_v2 as process
    from own_step_differentiable_geometry_v1 import OwnStepWheelGeometry
    from jump_process_geometry122_v1 import OwnProcessGeometry,wheel_bottom_velocity2
    from fudan_jump130_geometry_targets_v1 import bind_own_leg_targets
    assert Path(selected.__file__).resolve()==sources['137'].resolve()
    cores={'137':selected}
    for version in ('133','135','136'):
        ns={};exec(compile(trees[version],str(sources[version]),'exec'),ns);cores[version]=SimpleNamespace(**ns)
    versions=('133','135','136','137');calls={k:0 for k in versions};checks=[]
    PARTIAL_RESULTS['completed_check_groups']=checks
    PARTIAL_RESULTS['paired_core_evaluation_counts']=calls
    checks.append('pinned_four_sources_AST_inverse137_to136_only_residual_activation_and_amount_transfer_inverse_to135')

    own_contract=load(base/'job0/assets/import_contract.json')
    geometry=load(base/'source/OWN_STEP_WHEEL_GEOMETRY_FROM_R2_SOURCE.json')
    reference=load(base/'source/OWN_FUDAN_STEP_TERMINAL_GOAL_CANDIDATE_V2.json')
    fk=OwnStepWheelGeometry(own_contract,geometry,reference['joint_names'])
    pg=OwnProcessGeometry(geometry,fk.body_names)
    hips=torch.tensor([own_contract['joints'][n]['local_pos0'] for n in ('left_hip','right_hip')])
    lengths,own_proof=bind_own_leg_targets(fk,own_contract,hips,'cpu')
    witness=load(base/'source/OWN_JUMP130_LEG_GEOMETRY_WITNESSES.json')
    perm=[witness['joint_names'].index(n) for n in fk.names]
    q=torch.tensor(witness['extension']['row']['joint_pos30'],dtype=torch.float32)[perm][None]
    zero=torch.zeros(1,3);rot=torch.eye(3)[None];aux=torch.zeros(1,54)
    g0=fk((zero,rot,zero,zero,q,torch.zeros_like(q),aux))
    f=lambda x:torch.tensor([x],dtype=torch.float32)
    b=lambda x:torch.tensor([x],dtype=torch.bool)
    scalar=lambda x:x if torch.is_tensor(x) else f(x)

    def inputs(height=0.,root_vz=0.,vx=0.,front=.10,force=100.,history_free=None,
               wheel_vz=None,centre_vz=None,ready=False,qualified=False,crossed=False,
               active=1.,closure=0.,age=300):
        h,rv,xv=scalar(height),scalar(root_vz),scalar(vx)
        p=torch.stack((scalar(front)-g0['high'][...,0].amax(-1),torch.zeros_like(h),h-g0['low'][...,2].amin(-1)),-1)
        v=torch.stack((xv,torch.zeros_like(rv),rv),-1)
        g=fk((p,rot,v,zero,q,torch.zeros_like(q),aux))
        free=force<=1. if history_free is None else history_free
        local=g['centre']-p[:,None,:]-hips[None]
        mode=direct.modes(g['low'],g['high'],torch.full((1,2),float(force)),p[:,0])
        m=dict(wheel_bottom2=g['low'][...,2],wheel_high2=g['high'],world_velocity3=v,
            wheel_bottom_velocity2=wheel_bottom_velocity2(g,fk),wheel_centre_velocity2=g['centre_velocity'][...,2],
            world_angular_velocity3=zero,request_active=f(active),heading_cos=f(1.),upright_cos=f(1.),
            root_y=f(0.),body_yaw_velocity=f(0.),process_ready=b(ready),
            virtual_leg_length2=local[..., [0,2]].norm(dim=-1),own_extension_length2=lengths['extension'],
            own_tuck_length2=lengths['tuck'],nonwheel_clearance_m=pg.nonwheel_clearance(g),
            unloaded_both=f(float(free)),supported_any=f(float(not free)),
            task_lane_and_far_bound=(g['low'][...,1].amin(-1)>=-.6)&(g['high'][...,1].amax(-1)<=.6)&(g['high'][...,0].amax(-1)<=1.15))
        # Explicit structural measurement counterexamples, not dynamic q/dq
        # or actual force/contact trajectories. Geometry is never overwritten.
        if wheel_vz is not None:m['wheel_bottom_velocity2']=scalar(wheel_vz)[:,None].expand(1,2)
        if centre_vz is not None:m['wheel_centre_velocity2']=scalar(centre_vz)[:,None].expand(1,2)
        elif wheel_vz is not None:m['wheel_centre_velocity2']=scalar(wheel_vz)[:,None].expand(1,2)
        kw=dict(launched=b(qualified),crossed=b(crossed),closure_m=f(closure),public_age=torch.tensor([age]))
        return m,mode,kw,g

    def C_of(m):
        return (m['wheel_high2'][...,0].amax(-1)<.35).to(m['world_velocity3'].dtype)*direct.smooth01(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']/.005)

    def evaluate4(m,mode,kw,effort=1.,legacy=0.):
        out={}
        for version in versions:
            z=cores[version].evaluate(m,mode,f(legacy),f(effort),**kw);calls[version]+=1;out[version]=z
            assert torch.equal(z['total'],sum(z['selected_terms'].values()))
            assert set(z['selected_terms'])==set(contract135['selected_optimization_term_names'])
            assert torch.isfinite(z['total']).all()
            if bool((m['request_active']==1).all()):assert -1e-7<=float(z['total'])<=.190001
        old,mid,broken,new=(out[k] for k in ('133','135','136','137'))
        assert set(old)==set(mid)==set(broken)==set(new)
        assert torch.equal(broken['total'],new['total']),'136/137 forward totals must be identical'
        for term in broken['selected_terms']:
            assert torch.equal(broken['selected_terms'][term],new['selected_terms'][term]),term
        for key in broken:
            if key in ('total','selected_terms'):continue
            if torch.is_tensor(broken[key]):assert torch.equal(broken[key],new[key]),key
            else:assert broken[key]==new[key],key
        for term in mid['selected_terms']:
            if term not in ('fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry','closure_compliance_cost'):
                assert torch.equal(mid['selected_terms'][term],new['selected_terms'][term]),term
            if term not in ('fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry','fudan_line_z_real_wheel_ascent','closure_compliance_cost'):
                assert torch.equal(old['selected_terms'][term],new['selected_terms'][term]),term
        for key in mid:
            if key in ('total','selected_terms'):continue
            if torch.is_tensor(mid[key]):assert torch.equal(mid[key],new[key]),key
            else:assert mid[key]==new[key],key
        ext_delta=new['selected_terms']['fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry']-mid['selected_terms']['fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry']
        expected=ext_delta*(1-.5*new['closure_quality_cost'])
        assert torch.allclose(new['total']-mid['total'],expected,atol=2e-7,rtol=2e-5)
        assert old['total']<=new['total']+2e-7 and new['total']<=mid['total']+2e-7
        E=.5*torch.exp(-4*(m['virtual_leg_length2']-m['own_extension_length2']).abs().sum(-1))*m['supported_any']*(m['world_velocity3'][...,2]>.15)*new['before_edge_gate']
        A=(6*new['conservative_upward_velocity']).clamp_max(2.5)*C_of(m)
        residual=(E-A).clamp_min(0)
        expected_ext=.01*effort*residual*(1-mode['top_support'])*m['task_lane_and_far_bound']*m['request_active']
        assert torch.allclose(new['selected_terms']['fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry'],expected_ext,atol=1e-7,rtol=1e-5)
        return out

    def run(**kwargs):
        effort=kwargs.pop('effort',1.);legacy=kwargs.pop('legacy',0.)
        m,mode,kw,g=inputs(**kwargs)
        return evaluate4(m,mode,kw,effort,legacy),m,mode,kw,g

    def total_values(out):return {k:float(out[k]['total']) for k in versions}

    vz=f(0.).requires_grad_();out,m,_,_,_=run(root_vz=vz)
    assert m['nonwheel_clearance_m']>.005 and C_of(m)==1
    ground_grad={k:float(torch.autograd.grad(out[k]['total'].sum(),vz,retain_graph=True)[0]) for k in versions}
    PARTIAL_RESULTS['ground_common_velocity_derivatives']=ground_grad
    assert abs(ground_grad['133']-.0015)<1e-6
    assert abs(ground_grad['135']-.0615)<1e-6 and abs(ground_grad['137']-.0615)<1e-6
    assert abs(ground_grad['136']-.0015)<1e-6,'Retain actual136 zero-boundary cancellation regression'
    for k in ('133','135','136'):
        measured=probe136['actual_original_selected_core_evaluations'][0]['cores'][k]
        assert abs(ground_grad[k]-measured['dR_dcommon_vz'])<1e-6

    # Actual-FK inputs across the zero boundary. Current root and both wheel
    # velocities share a variable; these are source derivatives, not actions.
    zero_boundary=[]
    for rv0 in (-.0001,0.,.0001):
        rv=f(rv0).requires_grad_();out,m,*_=run(root_vz=rv)
        grad={k:float(torch.autograd.grad(out[k]['total'].sum(),rv,retain_graph=True)[0]) for k in versions}
        expected={k:0. for k in versions} if rv0<0 else {
            '133':.0015,'135':.0615,'136':.0015 if rv0==0 else .0615,'137':.0615}
        row=dict(common_root_and_both_wheel_vz=rv0,C=float(C_of(m)),totals=total_values(out),derivatives=grad)
        zero_boundary.append(row);PARTIAL_RESULTS['actual_FK_zero_boundary']=zero_boundary
        for k in versions:assert abs(grad[k]-expected[k])<1e-6,(rv0,k,grad,expected)

    # Isolated exact scalar controls expose the chosen autograd at E=A=0
    # and E=A>0 without overwriting actual geometry or fabricating contact.
    scalar_boundary=[]
    for E,u_tie in ((0.,0.),(.375,.0625)):
        for offset in (-.0001,0.,.0001):
            u=f(u_tie+offset).requires_grad_()
            A=6*u;clamp=A+(f(E)-A).clamp_min(0);relu=A+torch.relu(f(E)-A)
            deriv={'136_clamp':float(torch.autograd.grad(clamp.sum(),u,retain_graph=True)[0]),
                '137_relu':float(torch.autograd.grad(relu.sum(),u)[0])}
            assert torch.equal(clamp,relu),'All scalar forward amounts identical'
            expected_clamp=0. if offset<=0 else 6.
            expected_relu=0. if offset<0 else 6.
            assert abs(deriv['136_clamp']-expected_clamp)<1e-6
            assert abs(deriv['137_relu']-expected_relu)<1e-6
            scalar_boundary.append(dict(E=E,u=float(u),A=float(A),offset=offset,
                amount=float(relu),derivatives=deriv,
                scope='ISOLATED_UNCLAMPED_PRE_CREDIT_OPERATOR_NOT_ACTUAL_CONTACT_OR_FULL_REWARD_INPUT'))
    PARTIAL_RESULTS['isolated_exact_zero_and_positive_tie_controls']=scalar_boundary
    body_only=[]
    for front in (.10,.20,.33):
        for rv in (0.,.01,.15,.16,.3,1.,2.4):
            out,m,*_=run(root_vz=rv,wheel_vz=0.,front=front)
            assert torch.equal(out['133']['total'],out['135']['total']) and torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
            body_only.append(dict(front=front,root_vz=rv,totals=total_values(out),C=float(C_of(m))))
    wheel_grad=[]
    for u0 in (0.,.0001,.01,.05,.075,.0833333,.10,.14,.15,.30,.40,.60):
        u=f(u0).requires_grad_();out,m,*_=run(root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=u)
        grad={k:float(torch.autograd.grad(out[k]['total'].sum(),u,retain_graph=True)[0]) for k in versions}
        assert all(math.isfinite(x) for x in grad.values()) and grad['137']>=-1e-6
        if 0<u0<=.075:assert abs(grad['137'])<1e-6,'Expected amount-matching plateau must be reported'
        if .10<=u0<=.40:assert grad['137']>.059
        wheel_grad.append(dict(fixed_root_vz=.6,wheel_u=u0,derivatives=grad,totals=total_values(out)))
    for kwargs in (dict(root_vz=0.,wheel_vz=.6),dict(root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=2.,centre_vz=0.),
                   dict(root_vz=.6,front=.6,height=.27,force=100.)):
        out,*_=run(**kwargs);assert torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
    for wheel in (0,1):
        m,mode,kw,_=inputs(root_vz=.6)
        m['wheel_bottom_velocity2']=torch.full((1,2),.6);m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'][0,wheel]=0.
        out=evaluate4(m,mode,kw);assert torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
        m,mode,kw,_=inputs(root_vz=.6)
        m['wheel_high2']=m['wheel_high2'].clone();m['wheel_high2'][0,wheel,0]=.35
        out=evaluate4(m,mode,kw);assert torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
    assert torch.equal(run(root_vz=.3,ready=False)[0]['137']['total'],run(root_vz=.3,ready=True)[0]['137']['total'])
    checks.append('retained136_zero_boundary_failure137_ground_point0615_epsilon_positive_tie_body_only_exclusions_plateau_explicit')

    # Actual own FK establishes C; no nonwheel measurement is overwritten.
    _,near_m,*_=run(front=.20)
    assert near_m['nonwheel_clearance_m']<0
    zero_height=-float(near_m['nonwheel_clearance_m'])
    def inverse_smooth(value):
        low,high=0.,1.
        for _ in range(40):
            middle=(low+high)/2
            if middle*middle*(3-2*middle)<value:low=middle
            else:high=middle
        return (low+high)/2
    domains=[];extra_heights=[]
    for desired in (0.,.1,.5,1.):
        h0=0. if desired==0 else zero_height+(.006 if desired==1 else .005*inverse_smooth(desired))
        out,m,*_=run(front=.20,height=h0,root_vz=.6)
        actual=float(C_of(m));assert abs(actual-desired)<3e-5
        domains.append(dict(desired_C=desired,actual_C=actual,h=h0,nonwheel=float(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']),totals=total_values(out)))
        extra_heights.append(h0)
    partial_velocity_grad=[]
    for domain in domains:
        for u0 in (.01,.05,.10,.15,.30,.60):
            u=f(u0).requires_grad_()
            out,m,*_=run(front=.20,height=domain['h'],root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=u)
            grad={k:float(torch.autograd.grad(out[k]['total'].sum(),u,retain_graph=True)[0]) for k in versions}
            assert all(math.isfinite(x) for x in grad.values()) and grad['137']>=-1e-6
            if domain['desired_C']<=.1:assert abs(grad['137'])<1e-6
            partial_velocity_grad.append(dict(C=float(C_of(m)),h=domain['h'],root_vz=.6,wheel_u=u0,
                derivatives=grad,totals=total_values(out)))
    switches=[]
    heights=(0.,.001,.005,.01,.10,*report135['derived_partial_gate_heights'],*extra_heights)
    for h0 in heights:
        for u0 in (.01,.05,.075,.10,.15,.30,.60):
            for front in (.10,.20,.33):
                for qualified in (False,True):
                    common=dict(height=h0,root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=u0,front=front,force=0.,qualified=qualified)
                    pre,mp,modep,kwp,_=run(**common,history_free=False)
                    post,mq,modeq,kwq,_=run(**common,history_free=True)
                    for key in mp:
                        if key not in ('supported_any','unloaded_both'):assert torch.equal(mp[key],mq[key]),key
                    for key in modep:assert torch.equal(modep[key],modeq[key]),key
                    for key in kwp:assert torch.equal(kwp[key],kwq[key]),key
                    delta={k:float(post[k]['total']-pre[k]['total']) for k in versions}
                    assert delta['137']>=min(delta['133'],0.)-3e-7,(h0,u0,front,qualified,delta)
                    if float(C_of(mp))==0:assert abs(delta['135']-delta['137'])<3e-7
                    switches.append(dict(h=h0,u=u0,front=front,qualified=qualified,C=float(C_of(mp)),
                        actual_nonwheel_clearance=float(mp['nonwheel_clearance_m']),unload_deltas=delta))
    known=[x for x in report135['contact_switches'] if x['old_unload_delta']>=0 and x['new_unload_delta']<-2e-7]
    assert len(known)==12,'Original actual report must retain all12 newly negative cases'
    repaired=[]
    for row in known:
        common=dict(height=row['h'],root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=row['u'],front=row['front'],force=0.)
        pre,mp,*_=run(**common,history_free=False);post,*_=run(**common,history_free=True)
        delta={k:float(post[k]['total']-pre[k]['total']) for k in versions}
        assert abs(delta['133']-row['old_unload_delta'])<3e-7
        assert abs(delta['135']-row['new_unload_delta'])<3e-7
        assert delta['137']>=-3e-7,dict(original=row,deltas=delta)
        repaired.append(dict(original_actual=row,replayed_deltas=delta,actual_C=float(C_of(mp))))
    checks.append('actual_FK_C0_point1_point5_1_low_and_high_u_same_input_history_switch_all12_original_new_valleys_checked')

    spatial=[]
    for h0 in (.001,.01,.10,.27,zero_height+.001,zero_height+.0025):
        for x0 in (.312,.32,.33,.34,.348):
            for u0 in (.05,.10,.60):
                x=f(x0).requires_grad_();out,m,*_=run(front=x,height=h0,root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=u0)
                grad={k:float(torch.autograd.grad(out[k]['total'].sum(),x,retain_graph=True)[0]) for k in versions}
                assert all(math.isfinite(y) for y in grad.values())
                assert grad['137']>=grad['133']-3e-5,(h0,x0,u0,grad)
                spatial.append(dict(h=h0,front=x0,u=u0,C=float(C_of(m)),derivatives=grad))
    window=[]
    for x0 in (-.5,.10,.14,.148,.149,.15,.20,.30,.31,.32,.33,.34,.35,.36):
        out,m,*_=run(front=x0,root_vz=.3)
        window.append(dict(front=x0,C=float(C_of(m)),nonwheel=float(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']),totals=total_values(out)))
        if float(C_of(m))==0:assert torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
    boundaries={}
    for label,fronts,h0,free in (('guide_overlap_ground',(.148,.149),0.,False),
            ('riser_loaded_high',(.3499,.3501),.27,False),('riser_air_high',(.3499,.3501),.27,True)):
        values=[]
        for x0 in fronts:
            out,m,*_=run(front=x0,height=h0,root_vz=.6,force=0.,history_free=free)
            values.append(dict(front=x0,C=float(C_of(m)),nonwheel=float(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']),totals=total_values(out)))
        drops={k:values[0]['totals'][k]-values[1]['totals'][k] for k in versions}
        boundaries[label]=dict(inputs=values,drops=drops)
    assert boundaries['riser_loaded_high']['drops']['137']>.0249
    assert abs(boundaries['riser_air_high']['drops']['137'])<3e-7
    checks.append('approach_derivatives_against133_and135_actual_proxy_window_and_riser_discontinuities_retained')

    height_grad=[]
    for mm in range(5,65):
        h=f(mm/1000.).requires_grad_();out,*_=run(height=h,root_vz=.6,force=0.)
        assert torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
        assert torch.allclose(out['133']['total'],out['137']['total'],atol=1e-7,rtol=0.)
        grad=float(torch.autograd.grad(out['137']['total'].sum(),h)[0]);assert grad>0
        height_grad.append(grad)
    for h0 in (0.,.001,.01,.1,.26,.27):
        out,*_=run(height=h0,root_vz=0.,force=0.)
        assert torch.equal(out['133']['total'],out['135']['total']) and torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
    landing=[]
    for x0 in torch.linspace(.40,.58,61).tolist():
        out,*_=run(height=.27,root_vz=-.2,vx=.5,front=x0,force=100.,qualified=True,crossed=True)
        assert torch.equal(out['133']['total'],out['135']['total']) and torch.equal(out['135']['total'],out['137']['total'])
        landing.append(float(out['137']['total']))
    assert all(y>=x-1e-6 for x,y in zip(landing,landing[1:])) and landing[-1]>landing[0]
    out,*_=run(height=.27,front=.60,force=100.,root_vz=0.)
    assert out['137']['selected_terms']['supported_top_after_qualified_cross']==0
    assert out['137']['selected_terms']['quiet_top_after_qualified_cross']==0
    for legacy in (-.023,.12):
        out,*_=run(active=0.,legacy=legacy,root_vz=.3)
        assert all(torch.equal(out[k]['total'],f(legacy)) for k in versions)
    pc=process.code(ready_callbacks=torch.tensor([80]),air_callbacks=torch.tensor([16]),positive_vz=b(True),
        qualified=b(True),landing=b(False),crossed2=torch.tensor([[True,False]]))
    pc=process.advance_code(pc,public_age=torch.tensor([300]),callbacks=4,prepared=b(False),clean_air=b(False),root_vz=f(0.),
        previous_front2=torch.tensor([[.36,.25]]),next_front2=torch.tensor([[.25,.25]]),wheel_bottom2=torch.zeros(1,2),near=b(True),landing_corridor=b(False))
    assert not process.unpack_code(pc)['qualified'].any() and not process.unpack_code(pc)['crossed2'].any()
    checks.append('133_air_height_gradient_apex_moving_landing_nonrequest_literal_and_single_flight_reset_preserved')

    # Same declared own-FK structural paths as actual V2; no actor, force
    # calculation or physical contact trajectory is claimed. The supported
    # cycle prescribes root velocity without integrating its fixed pose.
    names=('static','supported_cycle','microhop_1cm','hard_rush','far_shuttle','vertical_highhop_loop','jump_then_top')
    span=float(g0['high'][...,0].amax()-g0['low'][...,0].amin())
    v0=math.sqrt(2*9.81*.35)
    touch=(v0+math.sqrt(v0*v0-2*9.81*.27))/9.81
    transit=(.35+span+.02-.10)/touch
    def path(label,n=240,initial_front=.10,speed=None):
        speed=transit if speed is None else speed;rows=[]
        for i in range(n):
            row=dict(height=0.,root_vz=0.,wheel_vz=0.,vx=0.,front=initial_front,force=100.,top=False)
            if label=='supported_cycle':row['root_vz']=2.4*math.sin(2*math.pi*(i%20)/20)
            elif label=='microhop_1cm':
                t=(i%10)*.01;vs=math.sqrt(2*9.81*.01)
                if 0<t<2*vs/9.81:row.update(height=vs*t-.5*9.81*t*t,root_vz=vs-9.81*t,wheel_vz=vs-9.81*t,force=0.)
            elif label=='hard_rush':
                front=min(.35,initial_front+.005*(i+1));row.update(front=front,vx=.5 if front<.35 else 0.)
            elif label=='far_shuttle':
                j=i%40;row.update(front=-.2+.005*(j+1) if j<20 else -.1-.005*(j-19),vx=.5 if j<20 else -.5)
            elif label=='vertical_highhop_loop':
                t=(i%60)*.01
                if 0<t<2*v0/9.81:row.update(height=v0*t-.5*9.81*t*t,root_vz=v0-9.81*t,wheel_vz=v0-9.81*t,force=0.)
            elif label=='jump_then_top':
                if i<12:row['root_vz']=-.2 if i<4 else 2.4*(i-4)/7
                else:
                    t=(i-11)*.01
                    if t<touch:row.update(height=v0*t-.5*9.81*t*t,root_vz=v0-9.81*t,wheel_vz=v0-9.81*t,force=0.,vx=speed,front=initial_front+speed*t)
                    else:
                        front=min(.60,initial_front+speed*t)
                        row.update(height=.27,front=front,vx=speed if front<.60 else 0.,force=100.,top=True)
            elif label!='static':raise ValueError(label)
            rows.append(row)
        return rows
    def path_values(rows,ready=True):
        pc=process.code(ready_callbacks=torch.tensor([80 if ready else 0]),air_callbacks=torch.tensor([0]),
            qualified=b(False),landing=b(False),crossed2=torch.zeros(1,2,dtype=torch.bool),positive_vz=b(False))
        seq={k:[] for k in versions};previous_front=None;last_force=100.;clearances=[]
        for i,row in enumerate(rows):
            item={k:v for k,v in row.items() if k!='top'}
            free=row['force']<=1 and last_force<=1
            m,mode,kw,g=inputs(**item,history_free=free,ready=ready,age=200+i)
            clearances.append(float(m['nonwheel_clearance_m']))
            if previous_front is None:previous_front=g['high'][...,0].clone()
            gates=process.process_geometry_gates(g['low'],g['high'])
            clean=free and bool((g['low'][...,2]>.005).all()) and bool((m['nonwheel_clearance_m']>.005).all())
            corridor=gates['landing_geometry'] & b(free or row['top'])
            pc=process.advance_code(pc,public_age=torch.tensor([200+i]),callbacks=4,prepared=b(False),clean_air=b(clean),
                root_vz=m['world_velocity3'][...,2],previous_front2=previous_front,next_front2=g['high'][...,0],
                wheel_bottom2=g['low'][...,2],near=gates['near'],landing_corridor=corridor)
            state=process.unpack_code(pc);assert process.valid_code(pc).all()
            kw.update(launched=state['qualified'],crossed=state['crossed'])
            out=evaluate4(m,mode,kw)
            for k in versions:seq[k].append(float(out[k]['total']))
            previous_front=g['high'][...,0].clone();last_force=row['force']
        return seq,{k:bool(state[k]) for k in ('ready','qualified','crossed','landing')},dict(
            min_nonwheel=min(clearances),first_nonwheel=clearances[0],negative_proxy_controls=sum(x<0 for x in clearances))
    def discounted(seq,n,repeat=False):
        values=(seq*((n+len(seq)-1)//len(seq)))[:n] if repeat else (seq+[seq[-1]]*max(0,n-len(seq)))[:n]
        return sum(.99**i*r for i,r in enumerate(values))
    tables={};sequences={}
    for label in names:
        seq,state,domain=path_values(path(label));sequences[label]=seq
        repeat=label not in ('jump_then_top','hard_rush')
        table={k:{str(h):discounted(seq[k],h,repeat) for h in (32,128,1800)} for k in versions}
        for k,prior_key in (('133','old'),('135','new')):
            for horizon in ('32','128','1800'):
                assert abs(table[k][horizon]-report135['synthetic_total_return_tables'][label][prior_key][horizon])<2e-5
        if label=='jump_then_top':assert state['crossed'] and state['landing'] and domain['min_nonwheel']>.005
        else:assert not state['crossed'] and not state['landing']
        tables[label]=dict(totals=table,process=state,own_geometry_domain=domain)
    ready0=path_values(path('jump_then_top'),False)[1]
    assert not ready0['qualified'] and not ready0['crossed']
    original_seq,original_state,original_domain=path_values(path('jump_then_top',initial_front=.22,speed=.5))
    assert original_domain['first_nonwheel']<0
    original_blocked=dict(domain=original_domain,process=original_state,
        totals={k:{str(h):discounted(original_seq[k],h) for h in (32,128,1800)} for k in versions})
    def short(seq,effort=1.,closure=0.):
        cost=min(1.,(closure/.002)**2);survive=(1-cost)**(1/32)
        return sum(.99**i*survive**i*effort*(1-.5*cost)*r for i,r in enumerate(seq[:32]))
    delays=[]
    for version in versions:
        peers={label:short(sequences[label][version]) for label in names[:-1]}
        for delay in (0,16,40):
            seq=sequences['static'][version][:delay]+sequences['jump_then_top'][version]
            for effort in (1.,.8):
                for closure in (0.,.0012):
                    value=short(seq,effort,closure)
                    if delay==40 and effort==1 and closure==0:assert abs(value-peers['static'])<1e-7
                    delays.append(dict(version=version,delay=delay,effort=effort,closure=closure,H32_jump=value,
                        competitors=peers,jump_beats_all=value>max(peers.values()),critic='UNKNOWN_NOT_INCLUDED'))
    command_edge_time=(.35-.10)/.5
    command_edge_height=v0*command_edge_time-.5*9.81*command_edge_time**2
    assert command_edge_height<.27 and .10+.5*touch<.35
    command_risk=dict(start_front=.10,own_span=span,peak=.35,vertical_v0=v0,descending_top_time=touch,
        structural_transit=transit,runtime_command=.5,front_at_top_time_with_command=.10+.5*touch,
        edge_time_with_command=command_edge_time,wheel_bottom_at_edge_with_command=command_edge_height,
        faster_fixture_does_not_resolve_task=True,not_a_physical_speed_limit=True)
    checks.append('four_way_full13_term_complete_paths_and_H32_delay_effort_closure_risks_current_command_gap_retained')

    # The selected wrapper imports the candidate core from the new source dir.
    import mod4_fudan_contact_phase_reward_candidate_v2 as api
    params=api.BoundOwnStepReward(**reference['metadata']['bound_own_step_reward'])
    m,_,kw,g=inputs(root_vz=.6,wheel_vz=.1,height=.005,history_free=False,force=0.)
    root=g['body_position'][:,0,:];local=g['centre']-root[:,None,:]-hips[None]
    mode=api.contact_and_step_modes(g['low'],g['high'],torch.zeros(1,2),root[:,0],f(.6),params)
    am=dict(m,flight=mode['flight'],takeoff=mode['takeoff'],virtual_leg_length=m['virtual_leg_length2'],

        command_yaw_velocity=f(0.),root_height=root[:,2],root_lateral_position=f(0.),

        projected_gravity=torch.tensor([[0.,0.,-1.]]),body_angular_velocity=torch.zeros(1,3),world_angular_velocity=torch.zeros(1,3),

    assert torch.equal(wrapped['total'],sum(wrapped['selected_optimization_terms'].values()))
    assert wrapped['selected_optimization_terms']['fudan_takeoff_extend_own_geometry']==0
    other=api.combined_reward(am,replace(params,extension_length_m=.9,tuck_length_m=.01),mode)
    assert torch.equal(wrapped['total'],other['total'])
    extrema=[float('inf'),-float('inf')]
    for h0 in (-.01,0.,.002,.005,.01,.064,.1,.26,.27,.4):
        for rv0 in (-1.,0.,.1,.3,2.4,4.):
            for force in (0.,100.):
                h=f(h0).requires_grad_();rv=f(rv0).requires_grad_()

# Actual V2 review: source135 NOT_SELECTED_FOR_RUNTIME

Root actually ran the frozen TEST_ONLY V2: seven groups PASS, 2756 evaluations per core, RNG unchanged. Report SHA `f4bd90d8e810315d6ae8eff18087a29f6e6c72d344e9512b6378c25d4e77a221` (94259 bytes). That PASS includes explicit adverse cases; it is not a task-success or universal reward-alignment result. The reviewer only read source/small JSON and authored this review; no project/AST/test/model/physics execution.

**Decision: do not select135 for runtime.** Its extension transfer newly makes some otherwise nonnegative same-pose contact-history transitions negative. Original135, V2, actual PASS, original v1 FAIL and probes remain unchanged. A separate source-only correction is authorized and still pending; this document does not admit any new runtime.

## Exact findings

1. **New contact-switch valleys, not all inherited.** In the actual report,76 cases have new unload delta<0:40 at C=0,23 at partial C,13 at C=1. Twelve have old133 delta>=0 but135 delta<0. This set includes both low-u/full-C and partial-C; it must not be described as twelve partial-C cases or merely inherited risk. Examples, all at fixed own pose/current inputs with only prescribed history changing:

   | front / wheel h / common u | C | old133 unload delta |135 unload delta |
   |---|---:|---:|---:|
   |.10 / .005 /.10 |1 |+.0013787076 |-.00091759115 |
   |.10 / .010 /.10 |1 |+.0017631333 |-.00053316541 |
   |.33 / .018836779 /.60 |.103991807 |+.00025132857 |-.00208848715 |
   |.20 / .020336779 /.15 |.499990344 |+.00028828345 |-.00171167962 |

   Source135 `jump_process_command_reward122_v1.py:58–60,68,78–79` prepays the common-wheel line signal while retaining some supported extension. On unloading, the prepayment is already present, while the residual extension disappears. This is a deterministic total-reward counterexample, not a measured physical contact sequence.

2. **Conditional takeoff improvement is real; its scope is limited.** Actual C=1 common rigid root/wheel-vz gradient at zero changes `.001500000013 -> .061500001699`. Body-only added value is exactly0. Nevertheless source135 lines52,68,77–78 retain root-up and supported-extension reward with stationary wheels. For aligned supported inputs, root-vz=.3, wheels stationary, extension target matched and before-edge=1, these inherited additions are `.00045+.005` before effort/closure. The fixture's supported-cycle return is unchanged (.542151 H32,1.899261 over1800). The cycle prescribes velocities while holding a static pose; it is not a kinematically integrated or physically feasible cycle proof.

3. **Near-ground geometry can close the added signal well before the wheel riser.** The own row9 extension shape at front.148 has C=1; at.149,guide-roller XY overlap assigns platform surface and C=0. At root/common-vz=.3 the total drops `.0339043364 -> .0209043361`, while old133 remains `.0209043361`. At front.20,h0..1cm,135 equals133. At front.33,h1/10mm,both have total dx=-.1875; at h.10/.27 with C=1,135 dx=0. At front.35 a still-loaded/high-clearance case loses .025. The authored-shape proxy does not establish actual PhysX/cooked collision. Removing its guard without evidence is unjustified.

4. **Full synthetic path ordering has explicit reversals.** New H32 values: nominal safe-start jump1.238105; body-cycle.542151; microhop.581361; hard-rush.825059; far-shuttle.639206; immediate vertical high-hop1.578292. Adding16 static controls makes task-jump.561084 at effort1/closure0, or.448868 at effort.8/closure0. The latter loses to the body-cycle as well. All12 `jump_beats_all=false` rows include the high vertical hop; they do not all mean losing to standing. The nominal task-jump wins H128/1800 among tested paths, but those paths assume preparation readiness80 and prescribed support. Ready0 never qualifies. The critic is omitted. Hard-rush eventually enters a negative proxy-clearance domain, so its structural return is not a reachable collision-free strategy proof.

5. **The faster structural path does not qualify command.5.** Safe-start front.10/fixed extension row9,peak.35,initial vertical speed2.620496,gravity9.81 uses horizontal1.013202968 to complete a top footprint. At horizontal.5,the descending platform-height time.394835s occurs at front.297418; at front.35,time.5s,wheel bottom is.083998. This is a fixed schedule/shape counterexample, not a physical velocity limit or impossibility proof. Reward clipping/reference2.4 is not a physical bound. Actual134 useful jump height and current-state domain coverage remain unknown; old131/132 playback must not substitute.

## Narrow correction to test separately

The initially considered factor `C*(1-S(u/.15))` cannot fix the two C=1/low-u new valleys and is **not selected**.

Use the actual reward amounts instead. Let `E=.5*extend*supported_any*I(root_vz>.15)*before_edge`, `L=min(6*u,2.5)`, `C=I(front<edge)*S(nonwheel/.005)`. Keep the line term `L*max(air,C)` and replace only extension by `max(E-L*C,0)`. In consistent loaded-history inputs the coupled amount is `max(E,L*C)`. On unloaded inputs extension is0 and line keeps the precredit; other air/height/tuck increments are nonnegative.

For the same pose/current-force/effort/closure and qualified state, before the edge `air_after=G<=C`. If old133 unload reward does not decrease, this amount-matched version cannot introduce a decrease: when E dominates it does not increase the pre-unload old amount; when L*C dominates the same amount survives unloading. Beyond the edge C=0 and before_edge=0, so the change vanishes. A full source/CPU fixture must verify these claims over the real own-FK domains and all13 terms; no new PASS is claimed here.

The tradeoff is explicit: with root already rising, low wheel-u and E>L*C, the coupled wheel-speed derivative may be0 until ascent credit catches E. This cannot retain135's earlier claim of strictly positive fixed-root wheel-speed gradients everywhere. Common root/wheel-vz near0 should retain.0615 because the original root-vz>.15 extension prerequisite is still false. Body-only cycles, C=0 behavior, hard geometry boundaries and short-horizon limitations remain. This is a local correction, not a promise to solve useful jumping.

## Minimal actual evidence for subsequent decisions

`MINIMAL_ACTUAL_INPUT_CONTRACT.json` specifies compact actual134 reward-input summaries, split by episode/public age/request mask and C/history/common-vz domain. No large raw/model reread is requested, no caller is authored, and no new physical guard is imposed. Root owns any new purpose, collection and execution. The information distinguishes whether preparation encounters C=1, partial-C or C=0 and whether a root-only rather than true-wheel ascent is actually present. It cannot by itself prove action-to-acceleration, world-model or critic accuracy.

All13 names/weights, nonrequest literal,17bit/six cues,20s/request200..1999,original2mm reset and physical same-flight/topcontact12/stable200 success remain unchanged. This review changes no runtime bytes. New files await root ordinary Git append.

"""New bounded CPU purpose; never calls an old fixture or reads models/raw.

Prescribed measurement/path controls are not dynamics, contacts or policy skill.
Only native137 source is evaluated; wheel-command kernels are diagnostics.
"""
import os
os.environ['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES']=''
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time
import traceback
from types import SimpleNamespace

START=time.monotonic()
PARTIAL={}
PURPOSE='SOL137_BODY_COMMAND_SOURCE_CPU_V1'


def main(args):
    import torch
    torch.set_num_threads(1)
    folder=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    base=Path(args.base_payload).resolve();f137=Path(args.source137).resolve()
    reads={};cache={}
    PARTIAL['input_read_ledger']=reads
    def data(path,limit=1048576):
        path=Path(path).resolve()
        key=str(path)
        if key in cache:
            reads[key]['in_memory_cache_uses']=reads[key].get('in_memory_cache_uses',0)+1
            return cache[key]
        if path.stat().st_size>limit:raise ValueError('Bounded input too large: '+str(path))
        raw=path.read_bytes();cache[key]=raw
        reads[key]=dict(bytes=len(raw),sha256=hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),explicit_integrity_or_JSON_reads=1)
        return raw
    def load(path,limit=1048576):return json.loads(data(path,limit).decode('utf-8-sig'))
    binding=load(folder/'SOURCE_BINDINGS_CPU_V1.json')
    for row in binding['own_source_files']:
        raw=data(folder/row['name']);assert hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==row['sha256']
    # Compile the pinned selected source bytes; no model loading occurs.
    core_path=f137/'source/jump_process_command_reward122_v1.py'
    core_raw=data(core_path)
    assert hashlib.sha256(core_raw).hexdigest()==binding['source137_sha256']
    inherited=load(f137.parent/'native135_Fudan_preedge_real_wheel_takeoff_reward_source_v1/ORIGINAL_SHA256.json')
    for row in inherited['inherited_runtime_sources']:
        raw=data(base/'source'/row['name']);assert hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==row['sha256']
    report137=load(f137/'reports/ROOT137_FIRST_FOUR_WAY_RELU_BOUNDARY_CPU_ACTUAL.json',4194304)
    assert reads[str((f137/'reports/ROOT137_FIRST_FOUR_WAY_RELU_BOUNDARY_CPU_ACTUAL.json').resolve())]['sha256']==binding['report137_sha256']
    assert report137['status']=='PASS' and len(report137['checks'])==7
    negative=report137['same_pose_prescribed_history_only_switches']
    adverse=dict(total_switch_rows=len(negative),negative137=sum(r['unload_deltas']['137']<-3e-7 for r in negative),
        negative133=sum(r['unload_deltas']['133']<-3e-7 for r in negative),
        newly_negative137_when133_nonnegative=sum(r['unload_deltas']['137']<-3e-7 and r['unload_deltas']['133']>=-3e-7 for r in negative),
        minimum137_minus_min133zero=min(r['unload_deltas']['137']-min(r['unload_deltas']['133'],0.) for r in negative))
    assert adverse['newly_negative137_when133_nonnegative']==0
    PARTIAL['same_denominator137_negative_switch_review']=adverse
    sys.dont_write_bytecode=True;sys.path[:0]=[str(folder/'source'),str(base/'source')]
    rng_before=torch.get_rng_state().clone()
    ns={};exec(compile(core_raw,str(core_path),'exec'),ns);core=SimpleNamespace(**ns)
    import direct_wheel_height_command_reward_v1 as direct
    import jump_process_frame122_v2 as process
    from own_step_differentiable_geometry_v1 import OwnStepWheelGeometry
    from jump_process_geometry122_v1 import OwnProcessGeometry,wheel_bottom_velocity2
    from fudan_jump130_geometry_targets_v1 import bind_own_leg_targets
    from wheel_command_reference_source_cpu_v1 import reference,tracking_diagnostics

    goal=load(base/'source/OWN_FUDAN_STEP_TERMINAL_GOAL_CANDIDATE_V2.json')

    for row in binding['base_geometry_files']:

        assert identity['sha256']==row['sha256']
    fk=OwnStepWheelGeometry(contract,geometry,goal['joint_names'])

    hips=torch.tensor([contract['joints'][n]['local_pos0'] for n in ('left_hip','right_hip')])
    lengths,proof=bind_own_leg_targets(fk,contract,hips,'cpu')
    perm=[witness['joint_names'].index(n) for n in fk.names]
    q=torch.tensor(witness['extension']['row']['joint_pos30'],dtype=torch.float32)[perm][None]
    z=torch.zeros(1,3);rot=torch.eye(3)[None];aux=torch.zeros(1,54)
    g0=fk((z,rot,z,z,q,torch.zeros_like(q),aux))
    span=float(g0['high'][...,0].amax()-g0['low'][...,0].amin())
    anchor=dict(start_front=.10,own_span=span)
    scalar_ref=reference(torch.tensor([200]),**anchor)
    touch=scalar_ref['touchdown_time_s'];transit=scalar_ref['forward_reference_m_s']
    calls=0;rows_evaluated=0
    def measurements(rows,wheel_variable=None):
        n=len(rows)
        f=lambda key,default=0.:torch.tensor([r.get(key,default) for r in rows],dtype=torch.float32)
        h=f('h');front=f('front',.10);rv=f('rv');vx=f('vx')
        p=torch.stack((front-g0['high'][...,0].amax(-1),torch.zeros_like(h),h-g0['low'][...,2].amin(-1)),-1)
        v=torch.stack((vx,torch.zeros_like(rv),rv),-1)
        zero=torch.zeros(n,3);qq=q.expand(n,-1)
        g=fk((p,rot.expand(n,-1,-1),v,zero,qq,torch.zeros_like(qq),aux.expand(n,-1)))
        forces=f('force',100.)[:,None].expand(n,2)
        free=f('free')
        local=g['centre']-p[:,None,:]-hips[None]
        mode=direct.modes(g['low'],g['high'],forces,p[:,0])
        m=dict(wheel_bottom2=g['low'][...,2],wheel_high2=g['high'],world_velocity3=v,
            wheel_bottom_velocity2=wheel_bottom_velocity2(g,fk),wheel_centre_velocity2=g['centre_velocity'][...,2],
            world_angular_velocity3=zero,request_active=torch.ones(n),heading_cos=torch.ones(n),upright_cos=torch.ones(n),
            root_y=torch.zeros(n),body_yaw_velocity=torch.zeros(n),process_ready=torch.ones(n,dtype=torch.bool),
            virtual_leg_length2=local[..., [0,2]].norm(dim=-1),own_extension_length2=lengths['extension'],
            own_tuck_length2=lengths['tuck'],nonwheel_clearance_m=pg.nonwheel_clearance(g),
            unloaded_both=free,supported_any=1-free,
            task_lane_and_far_bound=(g['low'][...,1].amin(-1)>=-.6)&(g['high'][...,1].amax(-1)<=.6)&(g['high'][...,0].amax(-1)<=1.15))
        wheel=f('wheel') if wheel_variable is None else wheel_variable
        # Prescribed measurement counterexamples, not dynamic joint velocities.
        m['wheel_bottom_velocity2']=wheel[:,None].expand(n,2)
        m['wheel_centre_velocity2']=wheel[:,None].expand(n,2)
        return m,mode,g
    def evaluate(rows,m,mode,launched=None,crossed=None):
        nonlocal calls,rows_evaluated
        n=len(rows);false=torch.zeros(n,dtype=torch.bool)
        out=core.evaluate(m,mode,torch.zeros(n),torch.ones(n),launched=false if launched is None else launched,
            crossed=false if crossed is None else crossed,closure_m=torch.zeros(n),
            public_age=torch.tensor([r.get('age',200) for r in rows]))
        calls+=1;rows_evaluated+=n
        assert len(out['selected_terms'])==13 and torch.equal(out['total'],sum(out['selected_terms'].values()))
        assert torch.isfinite(out['total']).all() and (out['total']>=-1e-7).all() and (out['total']<=.190001).all()
        return out
    body_rows=[dict(rv=rv,wheel=0.) for rv in (0.,.16,.6,2.4)]

    wheels=[dict(root_vz=.6,wheel_u=speeds[i],reward137=float(out['total'][i]),dR137_du=float(dr[i]),
        tracking_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY=float(qtrack[i]),dTracking_du=float(dq[i])) for i in range(len(speeds))]
    PARTIAL['fixed_root_wheel_speed_controls']=wheels

    names=('static','body_only_hold','rush_command_point5','microhop_1cm','clock_jump','delay16_jump','delay40_jump')

    for name in names:
        previous_force=100.
        for i in range(n):
            row=dict(age=200+i,h=0.,front=.10,rv=0.,wheel=0.,vx=0.,force=100.,top=False)
            if name=='body_only_hold':row['rv']=.6
            elif name=='rush_command_point5':row.update(front=min(.35,.10+.005*i),vx=.5 if .10+.005*i<.35 else 0.)
            elif name=='microhop_1cm':
                t=(i%10)*.01;v0=(2*9.81*.01)**.5
                if 0<t<2*v0/9.81:row.update(h=v0*t-.5*9.81*t*t,rv=v0-9.81*t,wheel=v0-9.81*t,force=0.)
            elif name.endswith('jump'):
                delay=16 if name=='delay16_jump' else 40 if name=='delay40_jump' else 0
                t=(i-delay)*.01
                if 0<=t<touch:row.update(h=2.4*t-.5*9.81*t*t,rv=2.4-9.81*t,wheel=2.4-9.81*t,
                    force=0.,vx=transit,front=.10+transit*t)
                elif t>=touch:row.update(h=.27,front=.10+transit*touch,top=True)
            row['free']=float(row['force']<=1 and previous_force<=1)
            all_rows.append(row);previous_force=row['force']
    m,mode,g=measurements(all_rows)
    lanes=len(names);false=torch.zeros(lanes,dtype=torch.bool)
    pc=process.code(ready_callbacks=torch.full((lanes,),80),air_callbacks=torch.zeros(lanes,dtype=torch.int64),
        qualified=false,landing=false,crossed2=torch.zeros(lanes,2,dtype=torch.bool),positive_vz=false)

    for i in range(n):

        low,high=g['low'][index],g['high'][index]

        free=m['unloaded_both'][index].bool()

        top=torch.tensor([all_rows[int(j)]['top'] for j in index])

            clean_air=clean,root_vz=m['world_velocity3'][index,2],previous_front2=previous_front,
            next_front2=high[...,0],wheel_bottom2=low[...,2],near=gates['near'],landing_corridor=corridor)
        state=process.unpack_code(pc);assert process.valid_code(pc).all()
        launches[index]=state['qualified'];crosses[index]=state['crossed']
        for k,name in enumerate(names):
            if bool(state['crossed'][k]) and first_cross[name] is None:first_cross[name]=i
            last_state[name]={s:bool(state[s][k]) for s in ('ready','qualified','crossed','landing')}
        previous_front=high[...,0].clone()
    out=evaluate(all_rows,m,mode,launches,crosses)
    ages=torch.tensor([r['age'] for r in all_rows])

    score=diag['equal_component_kernel_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY']
    assert torch.isfinite(score).all() and (score>=0).all() and (score<=1).all()
    discount=lambda seq,h:sum(.99**i*float(x) for i,x in enumerate(seq[:h]))
    paths={}
    for k,name in enumerate(names):
        s=slice(k*n,(k+1)*n);seq=out['total'][s];qq=score[s]
        paths[name]=dict(controls=n,source137_returns={str(h):discount(seq,h) for h in (32,120)},
            tracking_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY_returns={str(h):discount(qq,h) for h in (32,120)},
            source_process=last_state[name],source_first_cross_control=first_cross[name],
            minimum_actual_FK_nonwheel_proxy=float(m['nonwheel_clearance_m'][s].amin()),
            maximum_prescribed_wheel_bottom=float(m['wheel_bottom2'][s].amax()),
            exact_selected13_sums={key:float(value[s].sum()) for key,value in out['selected_terms'].items()},
            physical_success='UNKNOWN_SOURCE_ONLY_NO_REAL_CONTACT_OR_DYNAMICS')
        if name.endswith('jump'):assert last_state[name]['crossed'] and last_state[name]['landing']
        else:assert not last_state[name]['crossed']
    PARTIAL['new_clock_reference_structural_paths']=paths
    old_h32=[r for r in report137['H32_delay_risks'] if r['version']=='137' and r['effort']==1. and r['closure']==0.]
    assert calls==3 and rows_evaluated==850 and torch.equal(rng_before,torch.get_rng_state())
    assert time.monotonic()-START<120

        input_read_ledger=reads,source137_evaluate_calls=calls,prescribed_measurement_rows=rows_evaluated,
        source_process_4callback_batch_advances=120,RNG_unchanged=True,

"""Counterfactual wheel-reference diagnostics; no selected reward/action change.

The clock reference uses existing public age and a declared own-span anchor.
It does not write goals, observations, forces, process state or policy inputs.
No skill follows from a high diagnostic score; original physical success wins.
"""
import torch


def reference(public_age, *, start_front, own_span, edge=.35, target=.27,
              initial_bottom=0., initial_upward=2.4, gravity=9.81, margin=.02):
    age=public_age.to(torch.float32)
    t=(age-200)*.01
    discriminant=initial_upward**2-2*gravity*(target-initial_bottom)
    if discriminant<0:raise ValueError('Declared vertical reference cannot reach target')
    touchdown=(initial_upward+discriminant**.5)/gravity
    transit=(edge+own_span+margin-start_front)/touchdown
    if transit<=0:raise ValueError('Reference requires a pre-edge start anchor')
    flight=(t>=0)&(t<touchdown)
    request=(age>=200)&(age<2000)
    h=torch.where(flight,initial_bottom+initial_upward*t-.5*gravity*t.square(),
        torch.where(t>=touchdown,torch.full_like(t,target),torch.full_like(t,initial_bottom)))
    vz=torch.where(flight,initial_upward-gravity*t,torch.zeros_like(t))
    vx=torch.where(flight,torch.full_like(t,transit),torch.zeros_like(t))
    return dict(height=h,upward_velocity=vz,forward_velocity=vx,request=request,
        phase_flight=flight,phase_landing=(t>=touchdown)&request,
        touchdown_time_s=touchdown,forward_reference_m_s=transit,
        peak_bottom_m=initial_bottom+initial_upward**2/(2*gravity),
        initial_upward_m_s=initial_upward,target_bottom_m=target)


def tracking_diagnostics(m, ref):
    # Both wheel-bottom and wheel-centre velocities matter; root-only vz is
    # deliberately absent. Equal physical units/scales are explicit here.
    wheel_v=torch.minimum(m['wheel_bottom_velocity2'],m['wheel_centre_velocity2'])
    e_h=((m['wheel_bottom2']-ref['height'][...,None])/.27).square().mean(-1)
    e_v=((wheel_v-ref['upward_velocity'][...,None])/2.4).square().mean(-1)
    scale=max(float(ref['forward_reference_m_s']),.5)
    e_x=((m['world_velocity3'][...,0]-ref['forward_velocity'])/scale).square()
    score=torch.exp(-(e_h+e_v+e_x)/3.)*ref['request'].to(e_h.dtype)
    return dict(height_squared_error=e_h,wheel_velocity_squared_error=e_v,
        forward_squared_error=e_x,equal_component_kernel_DIAGNOSTIC_ONLY=score,
        no_landing_or_success_credit=True,no_selected_reward_or_action_change=True)

"""Root executes the frozen136 source fixture, without model/raw/SDK/SSH/GPU."""
from pathlib import Path
import ast, hashlib, json, os, subprocess, time

T=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
L=T/'MOD4_H1_OVERNIGHT_FUDAN_SUPERMIMIC_REPAIR_GOAL_20261027'
F=L/'native136_Fudan_amount_matched_extension_transfer_reward_source_v1'
R=T/'MOD4_H1_FUDAN133_LINE_Z_RESOURCE_V134_20261030/reports'
CPU='C:/Users/lulu/miniconda3/envs/env_isaaclab/python.exe'

def main():
    pins={'INDEX_SHA256.json':'7515aaf11c12eb155691d8bd101bbe1bb55159d9638eb78fb34e1d379aaff640',
        'source/jump_process_command_reward122_v1.py':'774d93ce6a7e9b3e2205151f00cc5170cef66b212d7a7053a01f541d0fc3dd02',
        'tests/cpu_fixture136.py':'65e97fbb78438f8858e077d37a071df3fc232133e406957d069c360dfb3d641f',
        'SOURCE_CONTRACT136.json':'25239a349d22f2814ead4cd3f935afad4ab210d9a50150f0b9f32e3324b3475d'}
    for relative,sha in pins.items():
        raw=(F/relative).read_bytes();assert hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()==sha,relative
        if relative.endswith('.py'):ast.parse(raw.decode('utf8'))
    out=F/'reports/ROOT136_FIRST_THREE_WAY_AMOUNT_TRANSFER_CPU_ACTUAL.json'
    assert not out.exists()
    command=[CPU,'-B','-X','utf8',str(F/'tests/cpu_fixture136.py'),
        '--base-payload',str(T/'MOD4_H1_FUDAN133_LINE_Z_RESOURCE_V134_20261030/payloads_v2/seed27'),
        '--baseline135',str(L/'native135_Fudan_preedge_real_wheel_takeoff_reward_source_v1'),
        '--fixture-v2',str(L/'native135_own_geometry_domain_cpu_fixture_v2_TEST_ONLY'),'--report',str(out)]
    with (R/'NEW136_ROOT_CPU_CONSUMED_BEFORE_EXECUTION.json').open('x',encoding='utf8') as f:
        json.dump(dict(epoch=time.time(),pins=pins,command=command,CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES='',
            source_only=True,old135_reports_untouched=True,no_model_raw_SDK_SSH_GPU_or_optimizer=True),f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)
    env=dict(os.environ);env['CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES']='';env['PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE']='1'
    stdout=R/'NEW136_ROOT_CPU_ORIGINAL_STDOUT.txt';stderr=R/'NEW136_ROOT_CPU_ORIGINAL_STDERR.txt'
    with stdout.open('xb') as so,stderr.open('xb') as se:
        process=subprocess.Popen(command,cwd=T,env=env,stdout=so,stderr=se)
        try:returncode=process.wait(timeout=180)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            with (R/'NEW136_ROOT_CPU_ORIGINAL_WAIT_TIMEOUT.json').open('x',encoding='utf8') as f:
                json.dump(dict(epoch=time.time(),own_PID=process.pid,command=command,
                    OS_exitcode_unknown=True,no_kill_or_relaunch=True),f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)
            raise
    actual=json.loads(out.read_bytes()) if out.exists() else None
    with (R/'NEW136_ROOT_CPU_ORIGINAL_RETURNED_ACTUAL.json').open('x',encoding='utf8') as f:
        json.dump(dict(epoch=time.time(),returncode=returncode,report=str(out),
            status=None if actual is None else actual.get('status'),stdout=str(stdout),stderr=str(stderr),
            source_only_no_runtime_admission=True),f,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)
    print(json.dumps(dict(returncode=returncode,status=None if actual is None else actual.get('status'),
        report=str(out),checks=None if actual is None else actual.get('checks'))))
    raise SystemExit(returncode)

if __name__=='__main__':main()
